2025-06-15T01:16:47.9627236Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9627406Z <pr_title>
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9627997Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9628849Z </pr_title>
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9629037Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9629175Z <pr_description>
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9630484Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9631593Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9631678Z ## Problem
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9632200Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9632624Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9632890Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9633171Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9633448Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9633722Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9633952Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9634072Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9634547Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9635136Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9635214Z ## Solution
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9635328Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9635422Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9635754Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9636041Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9636817Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9637680Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9638313Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9638604Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9638709Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9638991Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9639172Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9639264Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9639479Z ```bash
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9639975Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9640537Z ```
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9640633Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9640731Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9640957Z ```bash
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9641617Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9642576Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9642808Z ```
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9642900Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9643052Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9643292Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9643391Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9643996Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9644522Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9644603Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9644856Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9645547Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9646074Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9646577Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9647133Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9647457Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9647833Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9648326Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9648407Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9648514Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9648518Z 
2025-06-15T01:16:47.9648605Z </pr_description>
